The prior "appears to blit at full intensity" claim was incorrect speculation. | `src/render/webglDraw.ts` | `tile.cc:1256 tileRenderRoofsInRect()` | low | verified |
| RD16 | **Egg transparency implemented.** FIXED 2026-06-15, refined 2026-06-17 — Walls and scenery in front of the player become transparent so the player is visible behind them. Two modes: `'alpha'` (flat α, simpler) and `'egg'` (CE-faithful per-pixel mask via `egg.png`, **now the default**). Condition matches CE default case: `hexIsToRightOf(player, obj)` + `hexIsInFrontOf` within configurable hex radius (`setEggRadius()`). 2026-06-17 fixes: (1) egg anchor derives from the player's actual per-frame render position (`objectRenderInfo.x/y + frameWidth/2, frameHeight` — note: must use `frameWidth`/`frameHeight`, the trimmed per-frame bounding box, not `uniformFrameWidth`/`uniformFrameHeight`, the sprite-sheet's padded per-slot size, or the anchor reconstruction is off by half the padding delta) instead of raw hex position + a CE-style `+16/+8` corner-to-center fudge — DH2's `hexToScreen()` already returns a bottom-center/foot anchor (unlike CE's `tileToScreenXY` corner), so the extra fudge was shifting the egg one half-hex NW. (2) In `'egg'` mode the wall now defaults to fully opaque outside the small egg-texture footprint instead of blending with `u_alpha` (which previously tinted the whole qualifying wall with just a hole cut in the center). (3) `art/intrface/egg.png` as produced by the DH2 asset pipeline resolves the FRM's raw mask-intensity bytes through the normal Fallout palette — correct for sprites, wrong for mask data, since different intensity steps land on different (often non-gray) palette entries and show up as visible colored rings instead of a smooth gradient. Swapped in a clean alpha-channel mask (solid white RGB, shape in alpha) sourced from jsFO's `inc/egg.png`, and switched the shader to sample `.a` instead of `.r`. (4) `_loadEggTexture()` now cache-busts its image URL (`?v=timestamp`) — without it the browser could keep serving a stale cached egg.png across reloads even after the file on disk changed. **2026-06-18 fix — root-caused the asymmetric-growth bug**: user reported that approaching a wall in `'alpha'` mode made transparency grow on only one side instead of symmetrically. Root cause: CE's actual occlusion test (`object.cc:4949 _obj_render()`) is not a single fixed formula — it branches into 4 different combinations of `tileIsInFrontOf`/`tileIsToRightOf` (OR, AND, single-test, single-test-with-`OBJECT_WALL_TRANS_END`-override) depending on bits in the wall/scenery's own `extendedFlags` (`flags_ext`, read from the `.pro` file). `isEggObject()` previously hardcoded only the AND case for every wall regardless of orientation, which only happens to produce symmetric growth for one specific wall facing. Worse, DH2's proto extractor (`tools/proto.py readPRO()`) never had a parse case for `TYPE_WALL` at all — `extendedFlags` wasn't even being extracted, so implementing the 4-case branch wasn't previously possible without a pipeline change. Fixed: added `readWall()` to `tools/proto.py` (verified against CE's `proto.cc protoRead() case OBJ_TYPE_WALL` byte-for-byte: `extendedFlags`, `sid`, `material`, each a 32-bit int read in that order) and wired it into `readPRO()`; also reconstructed scenery's `extendedFlags` from its existing two 16-bit half-reads (`wallLightTypeFlags`/`actionFlags`, which together are CE's single 32-bit `flags_ext`, just split). `isEggObject()` now replicates all 4 CE cases, reading `obj.pro.extra.extendedFlags`/`obj.flags`, preserving CE's exact (object, dude) vs (dude, object) argument ordering per case since `hexIsInFrontOf`/`hexIsToRightOf` are not symmetric under argument swap. `proto/pro.json` was regenerated from the existing local `data/proto/*.pro` files (verified: item/critter/scenery/wall/misc counts unchanged, only the new `extra` fields added) — no pipeline re-run needed by the user this time. **Gotcha**: `globalState.proMap` is loaded via `cachedJSON()` in `main.ts`, which caches the parsed JSON in IndexedDB (`src/idbcache.ts`) with no content-versioning — regenerating `proto/pro.json` on disk has zero effect on an already-loaded browser session until that cache is cleared, since it keeps serving the old cached `extendedFlags`-less data forever. Same class of bug as the earlier `egg.png` HTTP-cache issue, different cache layer. Added `clearAssetCache()` console command (calls `IDBCache.nuke()`) and a `wallExtendedFlagsSample` field in `debugEgg()`'s output that flags a likely-stale cache (0 of N sampled walls have nonzero `extendedFlags`). **2026-06-18, second fix — 'alpha' mode still one-sided after the above**: user reported the 4-case fix resolved `'egg'` mode but `'alpha'` mode still only grew on one side (the NW-SE hex axis), and also affected walls behind the player, not just in front. Root cause: `tileIsInFrontOf`/`tileIsToRightOf` are not distance checks at all — they're `dx <= dy*k` line tests in screen space, i.e. a *fixed half-plane* through the player, whose boundary literally **is** the NW-SE hex axis. That's deliberate for CE/`'egg'` mode: it's asking "is this wall between the isometric camera (fixed diagonal viewing angle) and the player", which is inherently one-sided by design — Fallout's real egg effect is camera-facing-only, not a radial bubble. No combination of the 4 CE cases can ever produce a symmetric circle, because each is built from that same kind of half-plane test. `'alpha'` mode, however, is a DH2 invention (not a CE feature) meant to act like a symmetric "see-through bubble around me" — it was wrongly reusing the CE directional test as its qualification gate. Fixed by splitting `isEggObject()`: the CE 4-case branching (renamed `isCEOccludingWall()`) is now used only when `Config.ui.eggMode === 'egg'`; `'alpha'` mode uses a plain `hexDistance(player, obj) <= radius` check with no directional gate at all, making it genuinely symmetric in every direction as the user walks closer. Togglable via `Config.ui.showEgg`, tunable via `setEggMode()`/`setEggAlpha()`/`setEggRadius()`/`debugEgg()`/`clearAssetCache()` console commands. CE ref: `object.cc:4949 _obj_render()`; `obj_types.h:81 OBJECT_WALL_TRANS_END`; `proto_types.h:411 WallProto`; `proto.cc:1709-1717`. **2026-06-26 — confirmed working after cache clear**: `clearAssetCache()` (commit fa0fc2b) + page reload resolved all remaining symptoms. `debugEgg()` had been showing `extendedFlags = 0` for every sampled wall because `proMap` was loaded from a stale IndexedDB entry predating the 2026-06-18 code changes (see Gotcha above). After clearing the cache: non-zero `extendedFlags` values flow through `isCEOccludingWall` correctly, and walls on both sides of the player qualify for egg transparency as appropriate. No source changes required — `tools/proto.py` `readWall()` and `isCEOccludingWall` were already correct. **2026-06-30 — added two more selectable `eggMode` values for comparison/debugging.** `'ce-literal'` (`isCEOccludingWallLiteral()`) re-implements the same 4-case `extendedFlags` branch byte-for-byte with zero DH2 deviations, kept separate from the tuned `isCEOccludingWall()` so the two can be diffed live. `'bbox'` (`isBBoxOccludingWall()`) is a DH2-original, non-CE-derived alternative: screen-space AABB overlap between the player's and the wall's sprite rects, gated by the same `hexIsInFrontOf` draw-order depth test `Obj.ts`'s `objectZCompare` uses, instead of the bit-flag heuristic. `setEggMode()` accepts all five values (`'alpha'|'egg'|'ce-literal'|'bbox'|'beta'`); `eggDebug()` logs all three occlusion predicates per object side-by-side with `(DIFF)` flagging on disagreement, for spotting cases where the hand-tuned `'egg'` mode departs from literal CE behavior. See `wiki/extended_flags.md` for the full writeup. **2026-06-30, second update — renamed `'egg'`→`'dh2-egg'` and `'ce-literal'`→`'ce-egg'`** (now `'alpha'|'dh2-egg'|'ce-egg'|'bbox'|'beta'`) so the mode names read consistently with the `'dh2'`/`'derived'`/`'naive'` light-propagation modes added the same day. Also merged a fix from the parallel `egg` branch into `isCEOccludingWall()`'s `extFlags===0x2000`, different-hex-x branch: reverted DH2's hand-tuned `player.x > obj.x` gate back to CE-authentic plain `rightDudeObj`, since the local screen-space predicates can't actually distinguish inside-east from outside-south players for that wall orientation (CE's own `object.cc:4957` "TODO: Probably wrong" applies) and the CE-authentic form was found to behave better in practice ("works besides minor corner" per the source commit) than the DH2 heuristic it replaced. | `src/render/webglDraw.ts`, `shaders/fragment.glsl`, `src/render/webglContext.ts`, `src/config.ts`, `art/intrface/egg.png`, `tools/proto.py`, `proto/pro.json` | `object.cc:4949 _obj_render()`; `tile.cc tileIsToRightOf()`; `proto.cc protoRead()` | major | fixed |
| RD17 | **Lightmap sampled off the player's hex (light circle offset, one side brighter).** FIXED 2026-07-02 — the GPU world-lighting shaders reconstructed hex coords from world position with a single-affine formula (`hex_x = 150.0 − …`, `hex_y = … − 75.7`), but `hexToScreen` (`src/geometry/hexScreen.ts`) is a **per-column-parity** affine map (the `x>>1` floor makes even/odd columns differ: even `sy=6x+12y+11`, odd `sy=6x+12y+5`). The `−75.7` constant was the even/odd average of the true inverses (`−75.9375` even, `−75.4375` odd), leaving a ±0.2375-texel (~±5 world-px, **zoom-scaled**) `hex_y` mis-sample whose direction flips with the player's column parity — so the light circle sat off the player's hex with one side brighter. Data was symmetric (`lightingPlayerDebug` confirmed); only the sampling was displaced. Fix: parity-aware `hex_y` constant selected by `floor(hex_x+0.5)` column parity, plus corrected `hex_x` const `150.0416667` (=150+1/24); drives the round-trip `shaderHex(hexToScreen(tile))` error to exactly zero on both parities (verified numerically). `renderObject`'s `tile-y` `baseY` (`webglDraw.ts`) updated to the matching parity offset (`11.25` even / `5.25` odd), replacing the single `8.4`. CPU floor path (`webglLighting.ts` `hexFromScreen(scr.x−13, scr.y+13)`) uses discrete rounding and is unaffected (its `−13/+13` fudge left for a future audit). See `wiki/alignment.md §6` for the full derivation. **Follow-up 2026-07-02 (stripes):** the parity fix left `gl.LINEAR` blending texels across the hex column stagger (rectangular filter over a per-parity-staggered lattice → NW-SE stripes). Added selectable interpolation `setLightingBilinear('off'|'linear'|'column-center'|'hex-lerp'|'bicubic')` (persisted in `Config.engine.lightingInterpolation`, default `'hex-lerp'`): a `u_lightInterp` branch in both world shaders' shared `sampleTileLight`, with the CPU (`setLightInterpMode`) setting the texture filter each mode needs. `'hex-lerp'` does a 3-tap barycentric blend over the 3 nearest hexes in parity-free axial space — verified over all 40000 tiles (exact at centres, weights sum to 1, taps always hex-adjacent → no stagger). The old boolean `setLightingBilinear(true/false)` still works (→`'linear'`/`'off'`). See `wiki/alignment.md §7`. | `shaders/fragment.glsl`, `shaders/fragmentLighting.glsl`, `src/render/webglDraw.ts`, `src/render/webglContext.ts`, `src/config.ts`, `src/main.ts` | `tile.cc tileToScreenXY()`; DH2 `hexToScreen` | major | fixed |
| RD18 | **Animations wider than the GPU texture limit were never drawn. FIXED 2026-10-23** — `tools/frmpixels.py` put every frame of every direction in one row, so 293 sprite sheets were wider than 8192 px and 133 wider than 16 384 px (up to 74 976 px, `hflthrbd`); WebGL rejects such a texture (`texImage2D` → `INVALID_VALUE`, checked), so those animations — 67 `bl` and 51 `bf` burn deaths, 11 `bd` and a few others — showed nothing, everywhere, and more of them on GPUs with a 4096 / 8192 limit. Sheets are now a grid: one row per direction, rows wrapping at 8192 px (`hflthrbd`: 7952 × 2352); each frame's `sx`/`sy` and the sheet's `columns`/`rows` are in the image map, the shader picks the cell (`u_frame`/`u_numFrames`, `u_row`/`u_numRows`), and hit-testing, the inventory, barter and talking-head canvases read `sy`. Old single-row sheets still work. **Re-run the `export_images` stage.** Checked in the browser: player art and the burn animation drawn from the grid, all directions. | `tools/frmpixels.py`, `shaders/fragment.glsl`, `src/renderer.ts frameCell`, `src/render/webglDraw.ts` | — (DH2 sprite sheets) | major | **FIXED 2026-10-23** |
| RD19 | **The camera scrolled past the black map border without end on maps with a scroll-blocker ring.** `clampCameraPosition` (RD11) reverts a move whose clamped centre lands on a blocker tile to the *pre-clamp* centre, which is already past the limits, so each scroll step went a little further out (arvillag: west, north and south). New `[dh2] scroll_limit=1` (default; `Config.ui.scrollLimitToBorder`): after the blocker check the screen centre is clamped to the black border (`getActiveScrollBarBounds`), so the border reaches the middle of the screen at most; `scroll_limit=0` keeps the old behaviour. | `src/render/camera.ts clampCameraPosition`, `src/config.ts`, `src/ui_options/fallout2Cfg.ts`, `tools/fallout2Cfg.py` | — (DH2 addition; CE's clamp is a fixed grid inset, `tile.cc tileSetCenter`) | major | **FIXED 2026-10-23** |
| RD20 | **Scroll borders had to be set by hand on some maps (New Reno Stables: the south one).** CE has no border value: `tileSetCenter` refuses a scroll step whose centre tile holds a scroll blocker (the ring each map places around its playfield) or that takes the centre ≥ 480×400 px further from the player. DH2 instead clamped to a rectangle from the bounding box of all blockers (or the floor), which goes wrong where a map has several blocker groups (7 elevations; on `newrst` 58 blockers outside the ring moved the south border ~400 px down), and had no player limit. Ported both checks (`clampCameraCE`); the black border sits 320×190 px (the original view's half) outside the area the view centre can reach inside the ring the player is in (flood fill of the free hexes; it first sat around the outermost blockers, 1–2 hexes too far out). `[dh2] scroll_bounds=ce` (default), `=dh2` keeps the rectangle; `scroll_player_limit=0` / console `setScrollPlayerLimit(false)` drops the player limit. The border is chosen once per elevation (it jumped when the player stepped onto a blocker hex), and `[dh2] walk_limit=1` (default) keeps the player and NPCs from pathing past it (`map/pathfinder.ts`; exits outside it — `ncr2`, `redwan1` — stay reachable). | `src/render/camera.ts clampCameraCE / scrollRingBounds / walkBorderBounds`, `src/map/pathfinder.ts`, `src/config.ts`, `src/ui_options/fallout2Cfg.ts`, `tools/fallout2Cfg.py` | `tile.cc tileSetCenter`, `object.cc _obj_scroll_blocking_at` | major | **FIXED 2026-10-23** |

## 31. Lua Script Backend (pilot, DH2 addition)

Decompiled `.int` scripts running as Lua — see [`lua_scripts.md`](lua_scripts.md). Opt-in (`[dh2] script_backend=lua`);
the bytecode VM stays the default until the backend covers every script.

| ID | Description | File(s) | Reference | Sev | Status |
|----|-------------|---------|-----------|-----|--------|
| L1 | **3 of 1443 scripts stay `.int`** (was 25, 2026-10-19; 4 until 2026-10-21, when mitoilet was decompiled with its missing operand restored from the source, see L3). Shapes added to `tools/int2lua.py` (each matched exactly; an unknown shape still fails the whole script): `push_int <identifier offset>; fetch/store_external` — sslc's `x += 1` on an import, CE reads `value.integerValue` either way (9: bcgengrd, scbrahmn, sccargrd, scdthcla, scmrkgrd, scslvgrd, silocker, vatteam9, vsgteam9); a nested `if` jumping straight to where the enclosing block's closing `push X; jmp` goes (10: ncbisgrd, ncbismen, nccasbou, ncliljes, ncmormen, ncmyrgrd, ncramire, ncsalmen, ncwritee, modinn); `0x800F` detach, a no-op for a script without a parent program (genint); a call with fewer arguments than declared when the callee never touches its frame — CE `opPushBase` uses the pushed count (sccop `getArmedPartyMember`).<br>Stay bytecode: **barstow, dumar, surf** use the interpreter-library dialogue (`0x804C`–`0x8061`, CE `interpreter_lib.cc opSayStart…`), which neither backend implements; they are Fallout 1 leftovers no scripts.lst entry or map names. **mitoilet** (decompiled 2026-10-21): `description_p_proc` compiles `set_local_var(1)` without the variable number, so the bytecode pops a word from below its frame — in CE the `windowId` that `_setupCallWithReturnVal` pushed, 0 for a game script, i.e. the source's `LVAR_Examined`; the Lua writes that from the source, the VM since L18 too. | `tools/int2lua.py` | CE `interpreter.cc opFetchExternalVariable`, `opPushBase`, `opDetach`; `interpreter_lib.cc` | minor | partial |
| L2 | **VM: code after `gsay_end` is skipped when an option shows a `gsay_message`. FIXED 2026-10-19** — `ScriptVM.call` now tells a procedure that halted in `gsay_end` / `gsay_message` from one that returned: it keeps the frame (it used to pop a "return value" off it, eating the top local — taylor's `talk_p_proc` lost `LVar0` and moved the wrong inventory after the dialogue), and `ScriptVM.resume()` (called by `dialogueExit`, which first clears the dialogue as the Lua path does) continues every suspended procedure innermost first and pops each return value, as CE runs the dialogue inside `gsay_end`. Checked in game: the Elder's dialogue on the VM ends with both stacks back at their depth before the talk. | `src/vm.ts call / resume`, `src/scripting.ts dialogueExit` | `game_dialog.cc gameDialogEnter`; `interpreter_extra.cc opGsayEnd` | minor | fixed |
| L3 | **Constants are numbers — mostly fixed 2026-10-19.** `tools/int2lua.py` names global variables from `data/data/vault13.gam`, a map's own map variables from `data/maps/<map>.gam`, and — when SSL sources are present (`ssl/`: Black Isle's own, from the Fallout wiki's script library, preferred; `data/scripts_src/`: the Unofficial Patch's, by killap) — local variables (`LVAR_*`) and the map variables a script uses (`MVAR_*`) from the script's `.ssl` and its `#include`s, and PIDs from `itempid.h` / `critrpid.h` / `scenepid.h` where an argument is a PID or compared with `obj_pid`. The SSL names are the patched scripts', so a script uses a tree's names only when every procedure of the vanilla `.int` is in its `.ssl`; a number with two names is left as a number. From the same sources `define.h` / `animcomd.h` name the arguments that take them: STAT_, PCSTAT_, SKILL_, PERK_ / OBJECT_ / TRAIT_ (by the `has_trait` type, TRAIT_PERK/OBJECT/TRAIT from CE `CRITTER_TRAIT_*`), INVEN_TYPE_, METARULE_ / METARULE3_, FLOAT_MSG_, ANIM_, REG_ANIM_, DMG_, DAM_, MARK_TYPE_ / MARK_STATE_, HEAD_, BACKGROUND_, ELEV_, KILL_TYPE_. Message numbers stay numbers but get their text as a comment (`.msg`). Still numbers: combined bit flags, values two names share (HEAD_MARCUS / HEAD_OVERSEER). **2026-10-21:** the SSL resolver finds includes whose folder the tree lacks (`..\generic\ziLocker.ssl` → `ssl/scripts/`) and `#include NAME` via string defines; procedures count as declared under `#define` aliases (`def_description_p_proc`), as `call` targets and from `procedure X##…` templates; a number two names share takes the script's own define. Inferred names, marked `-- inferred:` in the Lua: an `.ssl` missing at most 3 of the `.int`'s procedures (another version of the script), a GVAR as the base of an index expression (`GVAR_SALVATORE_WARNINGS + 2 - 1`), an operand restored from the source (mitoilet). The `decompile_scripts` stage writes the variable list `data/scripts/variable_names.tsv` (every GVAR/MVAR/LVAR a script uses, its name and source, or why it stays a number). Scripts in scripts.lst: 18 546 uses named from a source, 67 inferred, 66 numbers (the source itself uses a bare number). **2026-10-22:** a number two names share takes the one the script's `.ssl` mentions (wtpltrp2 / wsterm3b MVAR 77); `/* */` comments, also an open one, are skipped (hcchad, wiretscn got their names); `NPC_REACTION_VAR`, `MAP_NOTIFY`, `GLOBAL_NOTIFY` name variable numbers nothing else names (their -1 default is dead code); `CURATED_NAMES` adds one map-evidence name (mcatkdog, inferred) and 22 hand-chosen names marked `-- guessed:` for the scripts no map uses (ccguard, cctech, ccmaster, test1). Now every variable of the listed scripts has a name: 18 589 from a source, 68 inferred, 22 guessed, reviewed in [lua_variable_names.md](lua_variable_names.md). | `tools/int2lua.py read_gam / SslNames / name_const / report`, `tools/setup.py decompile_scripts` | `vault13.gam`, `<map>.gam`, BIS `ssl/`, UP `scripts_src` | minor | partial |
| L4 | **Floats are doubles in Lua.** The VM rounds every float result to 32 bits (`Math.fround`, CE `float`); vanilla scripts barely use floats. | `src/lua/prelude.ts` | `interpreter.cc opAdd…opDivide` | minor | partial |
| L5 | **No mod load order. FIXED 2026-10-19** — `[dh2] mods=a,b` (or `play.html#script_backend=lua&mods=a,b`) lists mod folders in load order; `loadLuaScript` takes `mods/<mod>/scripts/<script>.lua` from the last listed mod that has it, else the generated `data/scripts/<script>.lua`, else the `.int`. A DH2 rule (sfall's `mods_order.txt` also reads its list top to bottom; which entry wins on a clash was not verified). Needs `script_backend=lua`; checked in game with two mods replacing the Elder's script in both orders. | `src/scripting.ts loadLuaScript`, `src/ui_options/fallout2Cfg.ts parseModList`, `src/config.ts scripting.mods` | — | minor | fixed |
| L6 | **VM differed from CE where the Lua backend did not. FIXED 2026-10-19** (found by `luadiff.html`, now `tools/luadiff.html`; now `0xc001` pushes `read32() \| 0`, and/or push 0/1 via CE truthiness `sslTrue`, `asText` writes a boolean as 0/1, and DIV/MOD by zero throw `VMFatalError`, which `ScriptVM.run` catches and logs like CE `programFatalError` — the procedure stops, `call` restores the stacks and returns 0; `vm.call` pushes arguments first to last; both stacks are capped at 0x1000 values like CE `programStackPushValue` / `programReturnStackPushValue`, so a procedure that recurses forever — nimyrdor's `use_skill_on_p_proc` calls itself, a Black Isle bug — stops with a fatal error instead of freezing the game):<br>- `push_int` (`0xC001`) reads its operand unsigned (`BinaryReader.read32` → `getUint32`): a script variable sslc initialises to `-1` starts as 4294967295, so `== -1` tests fail (kcsulik `PartyMemberBackground`). CE pushes a signed int.<br>- `and` / `or` (`0x803E`/`0x803F`) push JS `x && y` / `x || y`, i.e. an operand, not 0/1 (CE `opLogicalOperatorAnd/Or`): `1 + (a and b)` differs (dccmitty `Node015` float range, dcsheila `sex_cost` divides by 0).<br>- string `+` a comparison result prints `false`/`true` (comparisons push JS booleans); CE prints `0`/`1` (sccop, dcjoey debug messages).<br>- division / modulo by zero: the VM gives 0 / NaN and runs on; CE `opDivide`/`opModulo` call `programFatalError`, which stops the procedure (the Lua prelude raises an error, the call returns 0) (mcgrisha `mod(5, tile_distance(…))`).<br>- `vm.call(proc, args)` pushes `args` last-first; only the test harness passes arguments (it now reverses them). | `src/vm.ts` `0xc001`, `0x803c`, `0x803d`, `0x803e`, `0x803f`, `binop`/`arith` | CE `interpreter.cc opPush`, `opLogicalOperatorAnd`, `opAdd`, `opDivide`, `opModulo` | minor | fixed |
| L7 | **bhrnddst: an object passed as caps. FIXED 2026-10-22 by a script fix (deliberate deviation from CE).** Only `DMRV_Caravan`'s guard loop (reached from `Redding_DMRV_Encounter` and `Redding_Band_Encounter`; the other `Redding_*_Encounter` procedures are fine): Black Isle's source writes `give_money(Critter, item)` where `item` (`LVar3`) holds the hunting rifle `Create_Enemy` just made, so each guard gets `item_caps_adjust(Critter, LVar3)` — and `2 * LVar3` with Fortune Finder. Without the perk too. CE adds a garbage amount (pointer bits); the VM passed NaN (the caps stack became NaN); the Lua stopped the procedure with the perk (the slaves were never created). **Fix:** the generated Lua gives `random(10, 20)` caps (doubled with Fortune Finder), the Unofficial Patch's amount (`scripts_src/maps/bhrnddst.ssl`, the same as `DMRV_Highwaymen`'s guards) — `SCRIPT_FIXES` in `tools/int2lua.py`, marked `--[[ DH2 fix (L7) ]]` in the Lua, switchable with `[dh2] script_fixes=0`; see lua_scripts.md "Script fixes". The VM keeps the bytecode; `item_caps_adjust` now ignores an amount that is not a number (both backends), so its guards get 0 caps instead of NaN. Tested on bhrnddst: with the fix 4 critters, the gun guard 10–20 caps (2× with the perk); `luadiff` differs only at that call. | `tools/int2lua.py SCRIPT_FIXES`, `src/scripting.ts item_caps_adjust / dh2_script_fixes`, `src/config.ts scriptFixes` | CE `interpreter.cc opMultiply`, `interpreter_extra.cc opItemCapsAdjust`; UP `bhrnddst.ssl` | minor | **FIXED 2026-10-22** (script fix) |
| L8 | **Reading an unset LVAR / MVAR wrote 0. FIXED 2026-10-20** — `Script.local_var` / `map_var` stored 0 for a variable never set, so a read changed state; the Lua backend skips a pure read in `a and b` (int2lua `PURE`), so after such a read the two backends' LVAR tables differed (found by the story crawler: Hakunin's `local_var(LVAR_One_Reward)` behind `global_var(GVAR_KILL_EVIL_PLANTS) == 7`). Now a read returns 0 without storing it, as CE `scriptGetLocalVar` / `mapGetGlobalVar` (every variable exists as 0). `map_var` also no longer returns `undefined` on a map's first read. | `src/scripting.ts local_var / map_var` | CE `scripts.cc scriptGetLocalVar`, `map.cc mapGetGlobalVar` | minor | fixed |
| L9 | **`dialogue_system_enter` opened a dialogue inside the running one. FIXED 2026-10-20** — `Script.dialogue_system_enter` called `talk()` at once, so a script that restarts its dialogue from the code after `gsay_end` (Vault 15's Rebecca: `NodeTrans` → `do_trans` → `dialogue_system_enter`) ran a nested `talk_p_proc` while the first was still being resumed: the VM threw "return stack underflow" and the Lua backend dropped the second dialogue. CE `opGameDialogSystemEnter` only requests it (an inactive critter or combat refuses; else `gGameDialogSpeaker` + game state 4) and the main loop opens it after the script returns; now `dialogue_system_enter` sets a request that `Scripting.processDialogueRequest` (game tick) opens once no dialogue or combat runs. Found by the story crawler. | `src/scripting.ts dialogue_system_enter / processDialogueRequest`, `src/gameTick.ts` | CE `interpreter_extra.cc opGameDialogSystemEnter`, `game.cc` state 4 | minor | fixed |
| L10 | **`load_map` changed maps in the middle of the running script. FIXED 2026-10-20** — `Script.load_map` loaded the new map at once, so a dialogue option that ends with `load_map` (Redding's Hakeswill: `Node007a` takes him to jail → `load_map("REDDOWN.map", 1)`) tore down the map's scripts while that script and its dialogue were still running; the VM went on, the Lua coroutine lost its dialogue. CE `opLoadMap` → `mapSetTransition` only records it and the game loop changes maps afterwards (`mapHandleTransition`); now `load_map` records the transition (and sets GVAR_LOAD_MAP_INDEX as before) and `Scripting.processMapTransition` (game tick) loads the map once no dialogue runs. Found by the story crawler. | `src/scripting.ts load_map / processMapTransition`, `src/gameTick.ts` | CE `interpreter_extra.cc opLoadMap`, `map.cc mapSetTransition / mapHandleTransition` | minor | fixed |
| L11 | **VM: a procedure called while the same script was running cut the running one short. FIXED 2026-10-20** — `ScriptVM.call` did not save the interpreter's position: when an intrinsic of the running procedure called back into the same script (Vault City's bartenders: `Node014a` → `game_time_advance` fires the bar's own timed event → `vm.call`), the nested run left `halted` set and `pc` at its own end, so the rest of `Node014a` (the drink counter, `Node016`'s next reply) never ran and the dialogue closed. CE `_executeProcedure` saves and restores the program position around a nested run; `call` now saves and restores `pc`, the reader offset and the halted/aborted flags. Found by the story crawler (Lua was right). | `src/vm.ts ScriptVM.call` | CE `interpreter.cc _executeProcedure` | minor | fixed |
| L12 | **Every first visit to a map reseeded the RNG from the wall clock. FIXED 2026-10-20** — `Scripting.init` (called by `loadNewMap`, i.e. for a map not in the map cache) ran `randomInit(Date.now())`, so random rolls after entering a new map (critters a script places at random in `map_enter_p_proc`, goo dropped after `game_time_advance`, an object `RCLou` creates) depended on the clock; repeat visits loaded from the cache kept the sequence. CE seeds once per launch (`game.cc gameInitWithOptions` → `random.cc randomInit`; DH2: `random.ts` when the module loads); the per-map call is gone. This (not setTimeout-driven animations, which do not move anything while the frame loop is paused) made 11 story-crawler maps unstable run to run. | `src/scripting.ts Scripting.init` | CE `game.cc gameInitWithOptions`, `random.cc randomInit` | minor | fixed |
| L13 | **Map music kept a connection open after it stopped. FIXED 2026-10-20** — `HTMLAudioEngine.stopMusic` only paused the `<audio>` element and `playMusic` made a new one for every map, so each map change left a paused element that still had its `src` (and, while it waited for a user gesture to play, its HTTP request). After a few map changes the browser's six connections per host were taken and image loads stalled: map loads timed out. `stopMusic` now drops the element's `src`, as CE `backgroundSoundDelete` frees the stream; music restarts through `playMusic`. Found by the story crawler. | `src/audio.ts HTMLAudioEngine.stopMusic` | CE `game_sound.cc backgroundSoundDelete` | minor | fixed |
| L14 | **Objects scripted by their proto ran the script one before theirs in scripts.lst. FIXED 2026-10-20** — a proto's sid is a 0-based scripts.lst index (CE `proto_instance.cc _obj_new_sid`: `script->index = sid & 0xFFFFFF`, `scripts.cc scriptsGetFileName` → `gScriptsListEntries[index]`), but `Obj.loadScript` passed it to `lookupScriptName`, which takes the 1-based script number (`create_object_sid`, CE `opCreateObject` → `data - 1`). Every object that takes its script from its proto (items created or carried, not placed with a script in the map file) got the previous entry: Becky's book "The Lavender Flower" (sid 1171, `diRebBok`) ran `SCPeaon` (NCR peasants), whose `timed_event_p_proc` called `animate_move_obj_to_tile` on the book (`obj.walkTo is not a function`). Found by the story crawler (denbus2, Sheila: the VM threw out of the dialogue, the Lua runtime caught the error and went on). | `src/object/Obj.ts loadScript` | CE `proto_instance.cc _obj_new_sid`, `scripts.cc scriptsGetFileName` | major | fixed |
| L15 | **VM: a procedure that halted in `gsay_end` inside a nested call left its stack frame under the running one. FIXED 2026-10-20** — `ScriptVM.call` kept a halted procedure's frame on the shared data / return stacks for `resume()` and did not restore `dvarBase`. When the halt came in a nested call (Den's Sheila: the second session's `Node996` → `game_time_advance` fires the first session's timer → `timed_event_p_proc` opens the after-scene dialogue and halts in `gsay_end`), the outer procedure went on over that frame, and ending the dialogue resumed at a wrong return address (`RangeError: Offset is outside the bounds of the DataView`; also Metzger). A suspended procedure now takes its frame off the stacks and `resume()` puts it back at the depth it was called at (frame pointers are absolute indices); `call` and `resume` restore `dvarBase`. CE runs the dialogue inside `gsay_end` (`game_dialog.cc gameDialogEnter`) and each nested run keeps its own position (`interpreter.cc _executeProcedure`). Found by the story crawler (Lua was right). | `src/vm.ts ScriptVM.call / resume` | CE `interpreter.cc _executeProcedure`, `game_dialog.cc gameDialogEnter` | minor | fixed |
| L16 | **`.msg` files were parsed line by line; a missing script message threw. FIXED 2026-10-20** — both parsers (`Scripting` script messages, `loadMessage` game messages) took a line not starting with `{` as the continuation of the one before, so an indented entry was lost or glued to the previous one: miwell's only message (`  {100}`, "An old, boarded-up well…") was missing and its `description_p_proc` threw; combatai.msg 32444 read "::::belch!:::::" and 32445 did not exist. Now one parser, `data.ts parseMsgFile`, reads them as CE `message.cc messageListLoad` / `_message_load_field` does: three `{fields}`, anything outside braces skipped, newlines inside a field dropped, a malformed entry ends the load (cmbatai2.msg `{1382{}`, a file neither CE nor DH2 loads). Checked against the old parser on all 833 `.msg` files: the only other change is editor.msg 0, a `#`-commented entry CE reads too. A message number the file lacks (vccharly asks for 134) now gives "Error" with a warning, as CE `_scr_get_msg_str_speech` returns `_err_str`. Found by the story crawler's object pass. | `src/data.ts parseMsgFile`, `src/scripting.ts loadMessageFile / getScriptMessage` | CE `message.cc messageListLoad`, `_message_load_field`; `scripts.cc _scr_get_msg_str_speech` | minor | fixed |
| L17 | **Lua: a procedure that calls itself without end froze the game. FIXED 2026-10-20** — Black Isle scripts where a `#define use_skill_on_p_proc def_use_skill_on_p_proc` comes after the procedure of that name call themselves (New Reno doors nielddor, nisaldor, niwridor, nibishdr, nimyrdor: `use_skill_on_p_proc` → `use_skill_on_p_proc`; scofjack `talk_p_proc`; dccaesar `Node994` on every won Conversant roll). CE fills the program's 0x1000-value stacks and stops the procedure with `programFatalError` (the VM does the same since L6); Lua recursed until its own 1M-slot stack ran out, with every level's `obj_can_see_obj` line-of-sight checks: minutes of frozen page (any skill on Eldridge's doors). `tools/int2lua.py` now finds the procedures that can reach themselves through static calls and gives them `local _ <close> = recursion_guard()`; the prelude stops a script's recursion at 0x1000 / 3 levels with the error "Stack overflow." (sslc keeps 3 return-stack values per call; CE's exact depth also depends on locals and arguments, so a recursion with an effect on every level, like dccaesar's dice rolls, can still run a different number of levels — approximate). A coroutine that dies in an error is now closed (`coroutine.close`), so its guards unwind. The story crawler marks a fatal error the same way on both backends (`[FATAL Stack overflow]`, `[FATAL Division (DIV) by zero]`). Found by the story crawler's object pass (newr3, First Aid on Eldridge's door). | `tools/int2lua.py recursive`, `src/lua/prelude.ts recursion_guard / step` | CE `interpreter.cc programStackPushValue`, `programReturnStackPushValue`, `programFatalError` | minor | fixed |
| L18 | **VM: a procedure started without CE's call words. FIXED 2026-10-22** — CE `_executeProcedure` → `_setupCallWithReturnVal` pushes the program's flags, `checkWaitFunc` and `windowId` (0, null, 0 for a game script; `programCreateByPath` zeroes the `Program`) under the procedure's frame, and the exit stub pops them; `ScriptVM.call` pushed only the argument count. A well-formed procedure never touches them, but mitoilet's `description_p_proc` (its `set_local_var` lacks the variable number) takes the word below its frame: in CE the windowId 0 — local variable 0, the source's `LVAR_Examined` — in the VM a script variable. `call` now pushes the three words and drops them after the procedure returns, so VM, Lua and CE write the same variable. Found while investigating L1. | `src/vm.ts ScriptVM.call` | CE `interpreter.cc _executeProcedure, _setupCallWithReturnVal, opPopFlags` | minor | **FIXED 2026-10-22** |

## 32. Unofficial Patch (UPU) support

Opt-in: the pipeline's `build_mods` stage builds the install's `mods/upu.dat` into the mod folder
`mods/upu/`, used while `upu` is listed in `mods/mods_order.txt` (`src/modAssets.ts`). Details,
conflicts and what was checked: [`upu_support.md`](upu_support.md).

| ID | Description | File(s) | Reference | Sev | Status |
|----|-------------|---------|-----------|-----|--------|
| UP1 | **Not played on a real UPU install.** `build_mods` was run on a mock install with the real v34 `upu.dat` / `upu.ini` and the result loaded in the browser (maps, scripts, messages from `mods/upu/`, global scripts, hooks); a playtest of the changed areas, `luadiff` and the story crawler on UPU data are still to do. | `tools/setup.py build_mods`, `src/modAssets.ts` | — | minor | open |
| UP2 | **sfall global scripts. FIXED 2026-10-22** — the active mods' `data/scripts/gl*.int` run as in CE `sfall_global_scripts.cc` (start after a new game's map loads and after a load; repeat / type; map procedures), sfall global variables as CE `sfall_global_vars.cc` (saved), hooks after sfall (`register_hook_proc`, `get_sfall_arg`, `set_sfall_return`; `HOOK_INVENTORYMOVE`, `HOOK_GAMEMODECHANGE` fire), and sfall's virtual files (`fs_*`; an art FRM header edits the image's fps). All four of v34's `gl_k_*` scripts run (8-Ball fix, Dogmeat AI fix, Goris de-robing and walking speed options). | `src/sfallGlobalScripts.ts`, `src/sfallHooks.ts`, `src/sfallFs.ts` | CE `sfall_global_scripts.cc`, `sfall_global_vars.cc`; sfall `HookScripts.cpp`, `FileSystem.cpp` | minor | **FIXED 2026-10-22** |
| UP3 | **`mcjo` stays bytecode under the Lua backend.** A procedure-local variable without a constant initialiser — a shape `int2lua.py` does not decompile; it runs on the VM (the mod's `.int`, not the vanilla `.lua`: `loadLuaScript` checks the layers). | `tools/int2lua.py` | — | minor | open |
| UP4 | **sfall functions as far as UPU uses them.** `get/set_proto_data` map only offsets 96 (magazine size) and 388 (body type), `get_object_data` the Attack fields and an object's PID / SID / script index, `explosions_metarule` only 6; only two hook points fire (`INVENTORYMOVE` for the backpack, hands, armor slot and reloading — not dropping, picking up or containers; `GAMEMODECHANGE`); `fs_*` on art FRMs only changes fps / action frame; `set_ini_setting` holds for the page load (DH2 does not write the install's files); `tap_key` maps Escape / Enter / Space. Arrays are not saved with the game (as CE; sfall saves them). Others warn and return -1 / 0. | `src/scripting.ts`, `src/sfallHooks.ts`, `src/sfallFs.ts`, `src/sfallArrays.ts` | CE `sfall_opcodes.cc`, `sfall_arrays.cc`, `sfall_metarules.cc`; sfall | minor | partial |
| UP5 | **`sfall.dat`, `f2_res.dat` and the installer's `gl_ammomod.int` not used.** sfall's own scripts / interface art and the hi-res patch's art (`hr_*.frm`, `.edg`) are skipped: DH2 scales and zooms the original interface itself and has none of sfall's engine features; `gl_ammomod.int` (optional ammo formulas) lies loose in the install's `data/scripts`, which the pipeline does not read. Deliberate. | `tools/setup.py SKIPPED_MOD_ARCHIVES` | — | — | by design |
| UP6 | **UPU's map data with bad references.** reddown places a peasant whose art (critter FID 0x78) is past `critters.lst`: CE draws nothing; DH2's renderer needs an image, so `fomap` takes the critter proto's art — a deliberate deviation. Two objects (gecksetl, vctyctyd) name scripts past `scripts.lst`: no script, as CE. `fomap` used to abort the whole map on either. | `tools/fomap.py parseObject / getCritterProtoFID` | CE `art.cc artExists`, `scripts.cc scriptsGetFileName` | minor | deviation (documented) |

## 33. CE Parity Audit (2026-10-24)

Function-by-function comparison of every CE `.cc` file with DH2. Full tables, the MED / LOW findings and suggested
fixes: [`CE_PARITY_AUDIT.md`](CE_PARITY_AUDIT.md) and the 14 reports in [`ce_audit/`](ce_audit/). Only the HIGH
groups get rows here; the MED findings are tracked by their report IDs (e.g. `SCR-05`, `INV-07`).

The map-pipeline fixes (PA1, PA2, PA19's open doors) are in `tools/fomap.py`; since `maps/` is no longer in git,
an install picks them up by re-running the `export_maps` stage (`pipenv run python tools/setup.py <Fallout 2 folder> --stages export_maps`).

| ID | Description | Report IDs | Sev | Status |
|----|-------------|------------|-----|--------|
| PA1 | `tools/fomap.py` drops `data.flags` (locked / jammed), misc charges (money on the ground), key codes; door state not saved | ITEM-12, OBJ-02 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `tools/fomap.py` emits `dataFlags` (when set), door `openFlags`, misc `charges`, key `keyCode`; `Obj.fromMapObject_` sets locked / jammed from them and takes a ground money pile's amount from `charges`; `Obj.serialize` saves open / locked / jammed (map revisits and saves). The re-export (90 maps) showed **no vanilla map object has a lock or jam flag** and every map `charges` equals the proto's, so vanilla locks all come from scripts; the fix matters for mods and for the save state.** |
| PA2 | Ladders never change map: FO2 ladder `destinationMap` stored as `unknown1` | ITEM-13, ACT-07 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — fomap.py reads the FO2 ladder `destinationMap`; `Obj.use` follows CE (`!= 0` ladders, `> 0` stairs, builtTile −1 fails). 30 vanilla ladders lead to another map (e.g. Gecko junkyard → tunnels), checked in the browser.** |
| PA3 | NPC skills too low by `defaultValue` (`SkillSet.fromPro` / `get`) | SKILL-01 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `SkillSet.fromPro` stores CE `defaultValue` + the raw proto points (`withProtoDefaults`); older saves are migrated on load (`protoDefaults` flag).** |
| PA4 | No bonus-stat layer: drugs / radiation / armour / Gain-SPECIAL change Max HP and level-up HP / SP | STAT-01 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — drugs, radiation and perk effects (Gain-SPECIAL, armor perks, Lifegiver) go on `StatSet.bonusStats` (CE `critterSetBonusStat`); Max HP reads base ST / EN. Saves from before keep their old deltas in the base.** |
| PA5 | Level-up perk effects lost on DONE (Educated, Tag!, Here and Now); no Tag! skill pick | CHAR-01 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — the perk window calls back and the character screen re-copies the dude before DONE; Tag! asks PICK A NEW TAG SKILL and is taken back on cancel (CE `perkDialogHandleTagPerk`).** |
| PA6 | Trait skill modifiers only applied in the creator | CHAR-02 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `Critter.getSkill` and the character screen pass the dude's traits (CE `traitGetSkillModifier`).** |
| PA7 | `add_obj_to_inven` / `add_mult_objs_to_inven` add a clone | OP-03 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — the object itself becomes the entry (a joined stack takes the new object, as `itemAdd`), leaves the map; a carried item is refused; quantity clamped 1..99999.** |
| PA8 | `obj_is_carrying_obj_pid` counts stacks; no bags / equipped items | OP-02, INV-01, INV-02 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `inventoryWield.ts carriedQuantityByPid` / `carriedObjectByPid` (CE `objectGetCarriedQuantityByPid` / `…ObjectByPid`): quantities, bags, the dude's equipped items.** |
| PA9 | `destroy_object` on a carried item does nothing | OP-04, ITEM-15 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — the owner (any carrier, bags included: `findItemHolder`) loses it first, then `destroy_p_proc`; `destroy_mult_objs` uses the same search. Queued timers of the destroyed object are not cleared (unverified in CE).** |
| PA10 | `critter_dmg` ignores armour and flags 0x100 / 0x200 | OP-05, ACT-01 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — flags decoded; `_compute_dmg_damage` (amount − DT, − DR %, ≥ 0); the type picks the death anim. Still DH2: no message-window line, `self_obj` as source instead of CE's hidden dummy attacker.** |
| PA11 | `game_time` / `combat_is_initialized` are per-script snapshots | OP-01, SCR-02 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — both builtins read live (`vm_bridge.ts`: `gameTickTime`, `globalState.inCombat`); the per-entry-point copies are gone.** |
| PA12 | Dead companions stay in the party; party-count metarule off by one | COMBAT-03, CRIT-02, PTY-01, OP-06, PTY-04 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `critterKill` drops the member (`partyMemberRemove`), saves load without dead members; metarule 16 = 1 + live, visible critter members.** |
| PA13 | Companion Custom screen writes wrong area-attack / run-away values | PTY-02 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — "Always!" / "Sometimes" store `always` / `sometimes`; run-away choices map to CE's `_hp_run_away_value` index (Abject coward 0 % → min HP = max HP … Never! 100 % → 0), "You need a tourniquet" stores `tourniquet` (old saves' `none` read as tourniquet).** |
| PA14 | Return from Barter / Trade / Control re-runs `talk_p_proc` | DLG-01 (P6/P10) | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `gdialog_mod_barter` inside an option defers barter until the option's procedure returns (CE `_dialogue_switch_mode = 2`); `reenterDialogue` re-shows the same reply and options (CE `gdUnhide`) and ends the dialogue when there are none. Checked with Tubby (denbus1): back from barter on "Goodbye.", `talk_p_proc` not re-run. DLG-02 (`gsay_message` [Done]) is still open.** |
| PA15 | Primary burst / flame weapons fire one round | COMBAT-01 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `Weapon.isBurst` / `isContinuous` follow the current hit mode's attack mode (7 / 8), primary or secondary; continuous fire ported (`computeSpray` / `shootAlongPath`: 1 round per line and per critter, no path hits on the main target); the AI spray safety check and the player's burst AP cost follow the slot.** |
| PA16 | sfall critical-table fixes (`OverrideCriticalTable=2`, CE default) not applied | COMBAT-02 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `criticalEffects/sfallPatches.ts` (93 entries, generated from CE `criticalsInit`) applied over the static tables when ddraw.ini `[Misc] OverrideCriticalTable` is 2 / 3 (default 2); CriticalOverrides.ini (modes 1 / 3) is not read.** |
| PA17 | Unused-AP AC bonus not CE's model | COMBAT-04 (C22) | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `Combat.unusedApAC`: current AP while it isn't the critter's turn; full AP for every combatant at round start (`setMoveAll`); 0 for non-combatants and out of combat. `bonusAC` removed.** |
| PA18 | No AP limit / 3-AP cost for use, pickup, loot, skill in combat | ACT-02, ITEM-17 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `Critter.walkInFrontOfToAct`: in combat only on the player's turn, the walk must fit the move AP (charged), then 3 AP (proto.msg 700 when short) for use / pickup / loot; skill use walks AP-limited with no 3-AP cost. CE walks as far as the AP go and then fails; DH2 refuses the walk.** |
| PA19 | Open doors still block shots, light, placement, explosions | OBJ-01 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `setObjectOpen` → `setDoorPassable`: an open door gets OBJECT_OPEN_DOOR (0xA0000010), a closed one loses it, light rebuilt (CE `_check_door_state`). Doors the map leaves open (door.openFlags 0x01, 56 in vanilla) now load open on their saved frame (fomap.py emits it); before, they loaded closed.** |
| PA20 | Natural weapons use the wrong hidden flag; energy cells can't refuel the car | ITEM-01, ITEM-07 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — `itemIsHidden` reads the proto ITEM_HIDDEN flag (weight, no DAM_DROP, AI weapon choice); `critterKill` destroys hidden items (`itemDestroyAllHidden`); loot / steal views skip them. Small Energy Cell / Micro Fusion Cell on the car or trunk add 16000 / 40000 fuel × charge (`_obj_use_power_on_car`, msg 595 / 596).** |
| PA21 | Subtile metarule3 105 stub; entrances never auto-open; town map always shown | WM-01, WM-02, WM-03 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — metarule3 105 returns CE's 0 / 1 / 2 (`Worldmap.subTileVisitedState`), metarule3 101 marks subtiles; `Worldmap.markMapEntranceState` (CE matching, MAP_SAVED, raw −1 elevations) opens entrances on map load and elevation change and serves metarule3 104 (WM-04); the hotspot shows the town map only for a visited area with art, else loads the first open entrance (`wmAreaFindFirstValidMap`). Entrances with elevation −1 now load at the map header's start (WM-09).** |
| PA22 | VM backend: non-fatal exceptions stop the frame loop | SCR-01 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — any exception in a VM procedure ends only that procedure (`ScriptVM.run`, CE `programFatalError`); stack underflows, arg-count and procedure-lookup failures are `VMFatalError`s; a timed event leaves the list before it runs; `heart._tick` re-arms the frame first and catches a frame's error.** |
| PA23 | No worldmap / main-menu / `wind2` music | SND-01, WM-07 | high | **FIXED 2026-10-24 — world map `23world` / `20car`, main menu `07desert`, `wind2` for maps without music (`GameMap.playMapMusic`).** |
